教材原文全文8 段 · 718 词
原文逐段排列，点右侧按钮可看中文与旁注

Steve Jobs is a man who has great courage and dares to take risks. He may not be perfect but he pulled together the most fantastic act by overcoming Microsoft, the movie industry and the music business. Jobs followed his dream and led a revolution in the computing world. Steve Jobs was born on February 24,1955, in San Francisco, California. His biological mother was an unwed graduate student named Joanne Simp son and decided to put him up for adoption. Joanne had a college education, and she insisted that the future parents of her boy be just as well educated. Unfortunately, the candidates, Paul and Clara Jobs, did not meet her expectations: Clara didn't graduate from college and Paul only attended high school. When she found out that both his future parents had never graduated from college, she refused to sign the adoption papers. She only relented a few months later and agreed to have her baby adopted by them, under the firm condition that they would send him to college.

史蒂夫·乔布斯1955年2月24日生于加利福尼亚的旧金山。他的生母乔安妮·辛普森是未婚研究生，决定把他送人领养。乔安妮受过大学教育，坚持儿子未来的父母也要受过良好教育。不幸的是，候选人保尔·乔布斯和克拉拉·乔布斯没有达到她的预期:克拉拉大学没毕业，保尔只上过高中。发现孩子未来的父母都没有大学毕业，她拒绝在领养文件上签字，几个月后她才软化了态度.同意他们收养孩子，条件是要送孩子上大学。史蒂夫·乔布斯上了大学，但决定辍学，因为花销太大了。回忆起他在那里的时光，他说，“我那时候没有宿舍，所以就睡在朋友房间的地板上，我把可乐瓶子退回去，可以挣5美分押金，用来买食物。每周日晚上，我都要步行7英里穿过小镇，到赫利克利须那神庙好好吃上一顿饭。”20岁的时候，史蒂夫·乔布斯和一个朋友史蒂夫·沃兹尼亚克1976年4月1日在一个车库里开了一家公司。

Steve Jobs went to college but decided to drop out because it was too expensive. Recalling his time there he said,"I didn't have a dorm room, so I slept on the floor in friends' rooms, I returned coke bottles for the 5 cents deposits to buy food with, and I would walk the 7 miles across town every Sunday night to get one good meal a week at the Hare Krishna temple.

那年晚些时候，他们首次推出了AppleI，当地一家店提出要购买50台电脑。为了筹集资金来生产，他们不得不卖掉最贵的财产，乔布斯卖掉了他的大众面包车，沃兹尼亚克卖掉了他的惠普科学计算器。

At 20, Steve Jobs and a friend (Steve Wozniak) started a company in a garage on April 1,1976. Later that year, they debuted the Apple I and a local store offered to buy 50 machines. To finance the production, they had to sell their most expensive possessions. Jobs sold his Volkswagen van while Wozniak sold his Hewlett-Packard scientific calculator. Steve Jobs named their company Apple, in memory of a happy summer he had spent as an orchard worker in Ore gon. By 1982, however, his company sales sagged in the face of competition from IBM's new PC. Not wanting to dwell on successive failures, they worked on a new machine called the Mac in tosh. By 1986, the Mac, which Steve Jobs promised to be"insanely great," was a huge success. After 10 years, starting from 2 kids working in a garage, Apple computer had grown into a $2 billion dollar company with over 4000 employees.

乔布斯给公司起名为“苹果”，纪念当年在俄勒冈州当果园工人时度过的那个愉快的夏天。但是，到1982年的时候，面对IBM新开发的个人电脑带来的激烈竞争，他公司的销售下降了。不想总是沉溺于接连的失败，他们开始研发新的机器Macintosh。到1986年，史蒂夫·乔布斯承诺会“非常棒”的Mac，取得了巨大的成功。10年后，从两个孩子在车库开公司创业开始，苹果电脑公司已经成长为一家拥有4000多名员工、价值20亿美元的公司。然而，在30岁的时候，乔布斯被他与史蒂夫·沃兹尼亚克共同创办的公司解雇了。

At 30, Steve Jobs, however, was fired from the company he cofounded with Steve Wozniak. He left the company after losing a bitter battle over control with Apple's CEO John Scull ey, whom jobs had recruited from Pep si Cola. When Steve Jobs heard of the message he said,"You've probably had somebody punch you in the stomach and it knocks the wind out of you and you cannot breathe. The harder you try to breathe, the more you cannot breathe. And you know that the only thing you can do is just relax so you can start breathing again."

乔布斯在同苹果公司CEO约翰·斯卡利争夺控制权的激烈斗争中失败了，便离开了公司，约翰·斯卡利是当年他从百事可乐招聘来的。当乔布斯听到这个消息时，他说，“你可能挨了重重一拳打在你肚子上，它把你打得喘不过气来，你无法呼吸。你越是努力呼吸，就越不能呼吸。你知道，你唯一能做的就是放松，这样才能再呼吸。

Steve Jobs sold over $20 million of his Apple stock, spent days bicycling along the beach, feeling sad and lost, toured Paris, and journeyed on to Italy. Recalling this publicly heartbreaking episode, Jobs said,"I didn't see it then, but it turned out that getting fired from Apple was the best thing that could have ever happened to me. The heaviness of being successful was replaced by the lightness of being a beginner again, less sure about everything. It freed me to enter one of the most creative periods of my life."

"乔布斯卖掉了他的超过2000万美元的苹果股票，在沙滩上骑了几天自行车，非常伤心失落，先到了巴黎，后来又去了意大利。回忆这段广为人知的心碎经历时，乔布斯说:“我当时并没有意识到，但是结果表明，被苹果公司解雇，是发生在我身上最好的事情。成功的沉重没有了，取而代之的是再次成为新手的轻松，对凡事都不那么确定。它使我获得了自由，进入了一生中最具有创新力的时期之一。

During the next five years he started two companies -NeXT and Pix ar. NeXT didn't do as well as Jobs had dreamed for, but Pix ar was a success story. Jobs be came an instant billionaire. Meanwhile, his old company Apple was under immense pressure from its rival Micro soft and in 1996 posted billions of dollars in losses.

”在接下来的5年中，他创办了两家公司-NeXT和Pixar。NeXT没有乔布斯梦想的那么好，但是Pixar却非常成功。乔布斯立即成了亿万富翁。与此同时，他的老公司苹果承受着来自竞争对手微软的巨大压力，1996年公布损失了几十亿美元。

In December 1996, Steve Jobs convinced Apple to buy NeXT and make its software the foundation of the next-generation Mac OS. The technology he developed at NeXT became the catalyst of Apple's comeback. Initially appointed as Apple's adviser, Steve Jobs was named Apple's interim CEO in 1997.

1996年12月，乔布斯说服苹果公司收购NeXT，并使其软件成为下一代MacOS的基础。成为苹果公司东山再起的催化剂。乔布斯最初被任命为苹果的顾问，1997年又被任命为临时CEO.他又让苹果公司恢复了生机，把苹果公司带回为计算机世界贡献了新的创新技术的地位。

He had made Apple healthy again and returned it to a place where it was contributing new and innovative technologies to the computer world. Under his leadership, Apple returned to profitability and introduced innovations.

苹果恢复了盈利能力，并引入了创新。

本单元语言点 · 能直接搬到作文里的结构
  • be appointed as 被任命为（被动 + as）
  • under one's leadership 在某人的领导下（介词短语作状语，写作常用）
  • profit → profitable / innovate → innovative：名词/动词 + 后缀变形容词，完形补文高频
  • adopt（收养、采纳）vs adapt（适应、改编）——拼写只差一个字母，极易混
  • not...but... 结构表「不是…而是…」，用于给人物下定义
真题链接　这一单元的价值在语法而不在话题。「人生 / 励志 / 逆境」类文章常出现在阅读选择与概括段落中，而它的句子几乎每句都带一个可考结构：Little 置首的部分倒装、only to find 表意外结果、Having had... 完成式分词作状语、be elected + 职位（不加冠词）。2024.10 那套的完形补文里出现过类似的「形容词 / 名词 / 副词」词形转换，本单元的 limbless、motivational、wholeheartedly、uneventful 正是同一类构词。
写作素材　写「一个激励我的人 / 逆境与成长」类作文，最稳的骨架就是本篇：先用三个 no... 的排比把困境写足（No arms to..., no hands to..., no ability to...），再用 Little did he know 来一个倒装转折，最后落在态度上：What matters is not what we are given, but our attitude towards the struggles we encounter.
09

Facing Life's Challenges

直面人生挑战
必背 ★★★
为什么这个单元重要　两个极好的比喻——300 米跨栏、断弦的小提琴。它们不只是课文，是可以直接搬进作文的论证模型，而且覆盖「困难 / 坚持 / 局限 / 创造力」一整类命题。
Text A300 Hurdles
人生的跨栏要怎么过
记叙 + 议论 · 通篇比喻（把人生比作 300 米跨栏）
主旨：生活像一场 300 米跨栏：起跑位置各不相同，但距离一样；我们太在意输赢、太在意跟上别人，却很少活在当下。跨栏由易到难，最难的是最后一个——但正是它逼出你全部的力气。
段落骨架 · 按顺序读一遍就知道全文怎么走
①引子生活也许充满荆棘坎坷，但不要灰心绝望——也许某天它就勾勒出一幅美丽的画卷。今年我懂得了生活的真谛：生活就像一个 300 米跨栏比赛。
②第一层：输赢对所有比赛来说，最担忧的莫过于输赢。这就像我们在生活中担忧那些无关紧要的事。获胜真的那么重要？输了就意味着世界末日？
③第二层：当下我们担忧得太多，而活在当下太少。
④第三层：起跑在各自的跑道上蓄势待发。起跑地点不同，但每个人的赛程一样长——就像生活把我们领到不同地方、放进不同环境，却让我们最终在同一处谢幕。
⑤第四层：追赶枪响后我们都拼命追赶别人、想跟上他人的步伐。这说明我们总在担心「要适应别人」，而不是「成为独一无二的自己」。如果人与人没有区别，世界该多单调。
⑥第五层：第一个栏第一个栏最容易——刚开始还没筋疲力尽，我们轻松跨过。就像生活中那些我们总能解决的障碍。
⑦两种心态同一道栏可以有两种看法：「我跨不过去，太累了」或「我又跨过一个，离终点更近一步」。这正是在消极处境里看见积极因素的例子。
⑧第六层：中段第一栏与最后一栏之间的每一道，难度越来越大，你会觉得自己撑不住。生活中也常常如此：也许会被绊倒，也许踉跄而过，但无论如何你跨过去了，然后继续跑。
⑨第七层：最后 100 米你可能只想说「我跑不完最后 100 米」，但必须告诉自己一定能做到，而且要在临近终点时比开局跑得更快。
⑩收尾最后一个栏，是最难跨越的那一道。
必背长难句 · 8 句（原文 + 译文 + 结构拆解）
Life is like a 300m hurdle race.
生活就像一场 300 米跨栏。
拆　300m 是 300-meter 的缩写；hurdle 跨栏、栏架，引申为「障碍」——本篇全用这个比喻展开。
The first thing to any race is worrying about whether you are going to win or not.
任何一场比赛，第一件让人操心的事就是自己会不会赢。
拆　The first thing to… is + 动名词（worrying）作表语；whether… or not 引导宾语从句。
In life we worry too much and live in the moment too little.
生活里，我们担心得太多，活在当下的时间太少。
拆　live in the moment 活在当下；too much 与 too little 对照，后者修饰 live in the moment 的量。
We each start at different spots, but the race is still the same distance for everyone.
我们每个人的起点不同，但这场比赛对所有人来说距离是一样的。
拆　we each 后接动词原形复数（start）；spots 位置、起点；the same distance for everyone 对每个人都一样的距离。
You can look at that hurdle in two ways: "I can't make it over, I'm too tired" or "I am one more hurdle closer to finishing this race."
你可以用两种眼光看那道栏：「我跨不过去了，我太累了」；或者「我又过了一道，离跑完这场比赛更近了」。
拆　look at sth. in two ways 从两个角度看某事；make it over 越过去（over 是副词）；one more hurdle closer to 离……又近了一道栏（比较级前加数量短语）。
This is an example of taking a negative situation and seeing something positive in it.
这就是「把不利处境接过来，再从里面看出积极之处」的一个例子。
拆　an example of doing（介词 of 后接动名词）；take a negative situation 接受一个不利局面；see sth. positive in it 在其中看到积极的一面（positive 作后置定语）。
You may fall over that "hurdle" or struggle getting over it, but somehow you find it in yourself to jump over it and move on with the race.
你可能会摔在那道「栏」上，或者费很大劲才翻过去，但不知怎的，你总能从自己身上找到力气跳过去，继续往前跑。
拆　fall over 摔倒、绊倒；struggle doing sth. 费力做某事；find it in oneself to do 从自己身上找到做某事的勇气；move on with 继续进行。
Again, you must find it in yourself that you can do it, and you must finish the race stronger than when you had started it.
同样，你必须从自己身上找到「我能行」的信念；而且你必须以比开始时更强的状态跑完这场比赛。
拆　find it in yourself that... 与 find it in oneself to do 同源，it 是形式宾语；stronger than when you had started 是比较结构，than 后接省略的时间状语从句。
核心词块 · 10 词
词词性释义搭配 / 考点
hurdlen.跨栏；障碍clear a hurdle 跨过障碍
challengen./v.挑战face challenges 面对挑战
strugglen./v.挣扎；奋斗struggle to do sth 努力做某事
strengthn.力量；长处strengthen v. 加强
inspirev.激励inspiring adj. 鼓舞人心的
positiveadj.积极的≠ negative 消极的
overcomev.克服overcome difficulties 克服困难
distancen.距离the same distance 相同的距离
tripv.绊倒be tripped by sth 被…绊倒
finish linen.终点线cross the finish line 冲过终点线
教材原文全文9 段 · 579 词
原文逐段排列，点右侧按钮可看中文与旁注

Life may give you a negative, but don't despair - it may just develop into a beautiful picture one day. This year, I realized my theory that I have on life. Life is like a 300m hurdle race. Since I run the 300m hurdles, I would know what the race is like.

生活也许会否定你，但是不要绝望--也许有一天它会变成一幅美丽的图画。今年，我识到了自己对生活的理论。生活就像是一场300米跨栏比赛一样。

The first thing to any race is worrying about whether you are going to win or not. This relates to worrying about the petty things in life. Does it really matter if you win? Is it really going tobe the end of the world? Or will you be upset if you lose and then forget about it? In life we worry too much and live in the moment too little.

因为我跑300米跨栏，我知道这比赛是什么样子的。任何比赛的第一件事就是担心你是否会赢。这如同担心生活中一些琐碎的事情。你赢了真的重要吗?真的会是世界末日吗?或者，如果你输了会感到难过，然后慢慢忘了这件事吗?活中，我们担心得太多，而活在当下太少。

Next is the lining up in our own individual lanes. We each start at different spots, but the race is still the same distance for everyone. This relates to our lives taking us to different places and putting us in different situations, but hopefully we will end up in the same place, but maybe at different times.

接着就是在自己的跑道里排好队。我们每个人都在不同的地点起跑，但对每个人来说距依然是相同的。这就如同生活把我们带到不同的地方，置于不同的情况，但是我们有望最后到达相同的地方，但可能是在不同的时间。

Now comes the beginning of the race. We all try to keep up with everyone and pace ourselves with other people. This symbolizes that at times in everyone's lives we worry about fitting in or being the same as other people, instead of worrying about being unique. If everyone were the same, the world would be incredibly boring.

现在比赛开始了。我们都试着跟上大家，与他人保持步调一致。这象征着在生活中，我们担心自己是否合群，是否与别人一样，而不担心要保持独特。如果大家都是一样的，那么世界就会变得非常无聊。

Now comes the first hurdle. This hurdle is the easiest to get over because you are not worn out from running. We all make it over this hurdle with ease. This symbolizes those obstacles in life that we struggle to get over, but we always manage to do so. You can look at that hurdle in two ways:"I can't make it over, I'm too tired" or"I am one more hurdle closer to finishing this race". This is an example of taking a negative situation and seeing something positive in it.

现在来到第一栏前。这一栏是最容易跨过去的，因为你还没有跑得筋疲力尽。我们都不力地跨了过去。这象征着生活中的那些障碍，我们努力克服，但我们总能设法做成。你可以两个方面来看待这第一栏:“我太累了，跨不过去。”或者“我再跨一个栏就离完成比赛更近了。

The next part of the race is every hurdle between the first and last one. These hurdles get tougher and tougher and you may feel as though you can't go on. This happens a lot in life too. There may be a situation that arises where you feel as though you can't make it and all you can think about is giving up. You may fall over that"hurdle" or struggle getting over it, but somehow you find it in yourself to jump over it and move on with the race.

这是从消极的情况中看到积极的东西的一个例子。接下来就是第一栏与最后一栏之间的每一栏。这些栏变得越来越困难了，你感觉好像你无法坚持下去。这在生活中经常发生。可能出现一种情况，你感觉好像自己无法做到，心里想的全是放弃。你也许会被“那一道栏”绊倒，或者努力跨过去，但是不知怎地，你找到信心能跨过它，继续比赛。

Next is that last 100m of the race. You feel as though you just want to say,"No I can't do this." Again, you must find it in yourself that you can do it, and you must finish the race stronger than when you had started it.

接着是最后100米。你感觉好像你只想说，“不行，我做不到。”再一次，你必须在自己身上找到信心你能做到，你必须比刚开始的时候更坚强地完成比赛。

Now lastly is the last hurdle. This hurdle is the toughest out of all of them. In life we find ourselves falling and falling, and maybe getting too lost in our problems that we feel like we have been through enough and there's no hope. That last hurdle symbolizes these situations. It's one step close to the end of the race.

现在终于到最后一栏了。这是难度最大的。生活中，我们发现自己不断跌倒，也许在我们的问题中迷失太多，感觉自己已经受够了，没有希望了。最后一栏象征着这些情况。这离比赛结束还有一步。

Once you finish, you look back on it and you realize that you made it not by leaping, but step by step."Yard by yard, life is very hard. But inch by inch, life can be a cinch." All it takes is one step at a time, one hurdle at a time.

一旦你完成，你回顾比赛就会意识到你的成功并不是通过跳跃，而是通过一步一步。“码一码地，生活很艰难。但是一英寸一英寸地，生活就会很容易。”所需要做的就是一次走一步，一次跨一栏。

Text BA Violin with Three Strings
三弦小提琴
记叙 + 议论 · 一个现场事件，引出一句人生定义
主旨：1995 年 11 月 18 日，帕尔曼在林肯中心演奏，刚拉完几个小节，一根弦断了。所有人都以为他会停下来——他小时候得过小儿麻痹，走路要靠双拐，登台本身就是不小的成就。可他没有停：他等了一会儿，闭上眼，示意指挥重新开始，用三根弦把整首曲子拉完。那一晚的演奏比他用四根弦时更动人。他说：有时候艺术家的任务，就是弄清用剩下的东西还能奏出多少音乐。
段落骨架 · 按顺序读一遍就知道全文怎么走
①时间地点1995 年 11 月 18 日，小提琴家伊扎克·帕尔曼登台，在纽约林肯中心的艾弗里·费雪厅举办音乐会。
②登台本身即成就如果你去过帕尔曼的音乐会，你就知道：对他来说，登上舞台本身就是个不小的成就。
③身体条件他小时候罹患小儿麻痹症，所以走路要靠两根拐杖。
④观众的反应观众安静地坐着，看他穿过舞台走向椅子，开始演奏。
⑤意外发生但这一次出了问题。他刚拉完开头几个小节，小提琴上的一根弦断了。
⑥所有人的预期我们以为他不得不中止音乐会了。
⑦他没有可他没有。他等了一会儿，闭上眼睛，然后示意指挥重新开始。
⑧三弦的演奏乐队重新奏起，他从刚才停下的地方接着拉。那一晚他带着前所未有的激情、力量和纯净在演奏——而所有人都知道，仅凭三根弦不可能奏出和谐完整的作品。
⑨全篇最妙的一句这一点我知道，你也知道，但那一晚，帕尔曼拒绝「知道」这一点。
⑩现场他奏完时，厅里是一片令人敬畏的静默。随后人们起身欢呼，观众席的每个角落都爆发出非同寻常的掌声。
⑪那句话他笑了，擦去额上的汗，然后用一种平静、近乎神圣而不带半点自夸的语气说：「你知道吗，有时候艺术家的任务，就是弄清用自己剩下的东西，还能奏出多少音乐。」
⑫作者的反应这句有力的话，从我听到的那一刻起就一直留在我心里。也许，这就是人生的定义——不只对艺术家，而是对我们所有人。
⑬落点所以，在我们所生活的这个摇晃、剧变、令人困惑的世界里，我们的任务也许就是「奏乐」：先用手上所有的一切去奏，而当那不再可能时，就用剩下的去奏。
必背长难句 · 14 句（原文 + 译文 + 结构拆解）
On Nov. 18, 1995, Itzhak Perlman, the violinist, came on stage to give a concert.
1995 年 11 月 18 日，小提琴家伊扎克·帕尔曼登台举办音乐会。
拆　the violinist 是同位语，补充说明 Itzhak Perlman（人名后接同位语是英语新闻/传记文体的常见写法）。come on stage 上台（on stage 表「在舞台上」，不加冠词）。give a concert 举办音乐会（give 与 concert / party / lecture 搭配）。
If you have ever been to a Perlman concert, you know that getting on stage is no small achievement for him.
如果你去过帕尔曼的音乐会，你就知道：对他来说，登上舞台本身就是个不小的成就。
拆　have ever been to 曾经去过（现在完成时 + ever，问经历；have been to 去过，have gone to 去了未回）。getting on stage 动名词短语作从句主语。no small achievement 不小的成就 —— 这是「否定 + 反义」的双重否定修辞，比 a great achievement 更有分量（同类：no small matter 不是小事）。
He was stricken with polio as a child, and so he has braces on both legs and walks with the aid of two crutches.
他小时候患过小儿麻痹症，双腿都戴着支架，靠两根拐杖行走。
拆　be stricken with 罹患（stricken 是 strike 的过去分词）；braces 支架、矫形器；with the aid of 借助于。
They sit quietly while he makes his way across the stage to his chair.
当他一步步穿过舞台走向自己的椅子时，他们就安静地坐着。
拆　while 引导时间状语从句；make one's way across 穿过、走向；主从句都用一般现在时，描述的是每次演出都如此的惯常情形。
Just as he finished the first few bars, one of the strings on his violin broke.
他刚拉完开头几个小节，小提琴上的一根弦就断了。
拆　just as 正当……的时候（强调两个动作几乎同时）。bars 小节（音乐术语；bar 也可指「酒吧」「条」）。one of the strings 中 strings 用复数（琴弦共四根），谓语用单数 broke（one of + 复数名词，谓语跟 one 一致 —— 高频考点）。break 的过去式 broke，过去分词 broken。
Instead, he waited a moment, closed his eyes and then signaled the conductor to begin again.
相反，他等了一会儿，闭上眼睛，然后示意指挥重新开始。
拆　instead 单独使用表示「相反、而是」（前文说大家都以为他会下台）；waited / closed / signaled 三个动词顺承并列；signal sb. to do 示意某人做某事。
And he played with such passion and such power and such purity as they had never heard before.
他拉得那样饱含激情、那样有力量、那样纯净，是他们从未听到过的。
拆　such + 名词 + as 引导定语从句，as 在从句中作 heard 的宾语——such...as 连用是本句难点；三个 such 并列加强语气。
Of course, anyone knows that it is impossible to play a symphonic work with just three strings.
当然，谁都知道，只用三根弦是不可能演奏一部交响作品的。
拆　it 是形式主语，真正主语是 to play a symphonic work...；symphonic work 交响作品；with 表工具、手段。
I know that, and you know that, but that night Itzhak Perlman refused to know that.
这一点我知道，你也知道，但那一晚，帕尔曼拒绝「知道」这一点。
拆　全篇最漂亮的一句。三个 that 反复出现，句式极其简单，力量全在最后一个 refused to know —— 「知道」本是无法拒绝的，作者故意把它写成一种可以选择的态度，于是「拒绝知道不可能」就成了「不信不可能」。这种「把抽象动词当意志行为来用」的手法，写作里极值得学。
When he finished, there was an awesome silence in the room. And then people rose and cheered.
他奏完时，厅里是一片令人敬畏的静默。随后人们起身欢呼。
拆　awesome 令人敬畏的（此处是原义「让人心生敬畏」，不是口语的「很棒」）。silence 寂静（不可数）。rise 起身（不及物，过去式 rose，过去分词 risen —— 与 raise「举起」及物动词区分）。cheer 欢呼。
There was an extraordinary outburst of applause from every corner of the auditorium.
观众席的每个角落都爆发出非同寻常的掌声。
拆　an outburst of applause 一阵爆发的掌声（outburst 爆发，out- + burst）。extraordinary 非同寻常的（extra- + ordinary）。auditorium 礼堂、观众席。这句与前一句的「静默」构成强对比 —— 先静到极点，再响到极点，是现场描写最有效的节奏设计。
He smiled, wiped the sweat from his brow, raised his bow to quiet us, and then he said—not boastfully, but in a quiet, pensive, reverent tone—"You know, sometimes it is the artist's task to find out how much music you can still make with what you have left."
他笑了笑，擦去额头的汗，举起琴弓示意我们安静，然后说——不是自夸，而是用一种安静、沉思、虔敬的语调——「你知道吗，有时候艺术家的任务，就是弄清用剩下的东西还能奏出多少音乐。」
拆　smiled / wiped / raised / said 四个动作顺承并列；破折号之间是插入语，补充说话的方式；it is the artist's task to do 中 it 是形式主语；what you have left 是宾语从句，「你还剩下的东西」。
Perhaps that is the definition of life—not just for artists, but for all of us.
也许这就是生活的定义——不只是对艺术家而言，而是对我们所有人。
拆　not just...but... 不只……而且……；破折号后的介词短语补充说明 definition 的适用范围。
So, perhaps our task in this shaky, fast-changing, bewildering world in which we live is to make music, at first with all that we have, and then, when that is no longer possible, to make music with what we have left.
所以，在我们所生活的这个摇晃、剧变、令人困惑的世界里，我们的任务也许就是「奏乐」：先用手上所有的一切去奏，而当那不再可能时，就用剩下的去奏。
拆　全篇收尾的长句，主干只有 our task is to make music。in this shaky, fast-changing, bewildering world 三个形容词并列修饰 world（shaky 摇晃的、不稳的；bewildering 令人困惑的）。in which we live 是「介词 + which」引导的定语从句修饰 world（= which we live in，但介词提前更正式）。at first... and then... 两个时间层次。when that is no longer possible 是插入的时间状语从句。with what we have left 与 with all that we have 前后照应，构成「全部 → 剩余」的收束。

教材原文全文7 段 · 654 词
原文逐段排列，点右侧按钮可看中文与旁注

On Nov.18,1995, Itzhak Perlman, the violinist, came on stage to give a concert at Avery Fisher Hall at Lincoln Center in New York City.

1995年11月18日，提琴家伊扎克帕尔曼登上了舞台，他要在纽约林肯中心的艾弗费雪厅举办一场音乐会。

If you have ever been to a Perlman concert, you know that getting on stage is no small achievement for him. He was stricken with polio as a child, and so he has braces on both legs and walks with the aid of two crutches. To see him walk across the stage one step at a time, painfully and slowly, is an awesome sight.

如果你曾经听过帕尔曼的音乐会，就会知道登上舞台对他来说是不小的成就。他小的时候患过小儿麻痹症，双腿上都带着支架，要拄着双拐走路。看着他一步一步痛苦地、缓慢地，走上舞台，情景实在令人惊叹。

He walks painfully, yet majestically, until he reaches his chair. Then he sits down, slowly puts his crutches on the floor, undoes the clasps on his legs, tucks one foot back and extends the other foot forward. Then he bends down and picks up the violin, puts it under his chin, nods to the conductor and proceeds to play.

他痛苦地走到椅子那儿，步伐中透着威严。然后慢慢坐下来，把拐杖放在地上，解开腿的搭扣，一只脚向后缩，另一只脚向前伸。他弯下腰，拿起小提琴，放在下巴下面，冲着指挥点了点头，然后开始表演。

By now, the audience is used to this ritual. They sit quietly while he makes his way across the stage to his chair. They remain reverently silent while he undoes the clasps on his legs. The wait until he is ready to play.

到现在，观众已经习惯了这种仪式。他们静静地坐着，当他穿过舞台走向他的椅子时。解开腿上的扣环时，观众恭敬地保持安静。他们等待着他准备好演奏。

But this time, something went wrong. Just as he finished the first few bars, one of the strings on his violin broke. You could hear it snap - it went off like gunfire across the room There was no mistaking what that sound meant. There was no mistaking what he had to do.

但是这一次出了点儿问题。他刚刚演奏完前面几小节，他小提琴上的一根弦断了。你可以听到啪的一声--像枪声一样响彻整个房间。这个声音意味着什么很清楚，他必须得做什么也很清楚。

We figured that he would have to get up put on the clasps again, pick up the crutches and limp his way off stage -to either find another violin or else find another string for this one But he didn't. Instead, he waited a moment, closed his eyes and then signaled the conductor to begin again. The orchestra be gan, and he played from where he had left off. And he played with such passion and such power and such purity as they had never heard before.

我们以为他会站起身来，重新系上搭扣，拿起拐杖，一瘸一拐地走下舞台--要么重新找一把小提琴，要么为这把小提琴找另一根弦。但是他没有。相反，他等了一会儿，闭上眼睛然后示意指挥重新开始。乐队开始演奏，他从刚才停止的地方开始演奏。他的演奏充满了激情、力量和纯洁，就像他们从未听到过一样。

Of course, anyone knows that it is impossible to play a symphonic work with just three strings. I know that, and you know that, but that night Itzhak Perlman refused to know that. You could see him modulating, changing, re-composing the piece in his head. At one point, it sounded like he was de-tuning the strings to get new sounds from them that they had never made before. When he finished, there was an awesome silence in the room. And then people rose and cheered. There was an extraordinary outburst of applause from every corner of the auditorium We were all on our feet, screaming and cheering, doing everything we could to show how much we appreciated what he had done. He smiled, wiped the sweat from his brow, raised his bow to quiet us, and then he said - not boastfully, but in a quiet, pensive, reverent tone -"You know, sometimes it is the artist's task to find out how much music you can still make with what you have left." What a powerful line that is! It has stayed in my mind ever since I heard it. And who knows? Perhaps that is the definition of life - not just for artists, but for all of us. Here is a man who has prepared all his life to make music on a violin with four strings, who, all of a sudden, in the middle of a concert, finds himself with only three strings; so he makes music with three strings, and the music he made that night with just three strings was more beautiful, more sacred, more memorable, than any that he had ever made before, when he had four strings. So, perhaps our task in this shaky, fast-changing, bewildering world in which we live is to make music, at first with all that we have, and then, when that is no longer possible, to make music with what we have left.

当然，任何人都知道只用三根弦演奏交响乐是不可能的。我知道，你知道，但是那一晚扎克·帕尔曼拒绝知道。你可以看到他调整，改变，在头脑里重新作曲。一度，听起来好像他是在调弦为了得到琴弦从未发出过的新的声音。当他演奏完，房间里充满敬畏的寂静。接着，人们站起来欢呼。观众席的每一个角落都爆发出热烈的掌声。我们都站着，尖叫着、欢呼着，尽我们所能来表示我们有多么欣赏他所做的一切。他笑了，擦去额头的汗水，举起琴弓让我们安静下来。然后他说--不是自夸地，而是用平静的、沉思的、恭敬的语气--“你们知道，有时这是艺术家的任务，看看用剩余的东西还能创作多少音乐。”多么有力的话啊!自从我听到它，它就一直留在我的脑海里。谁知道呢?也许这就是生命的定义--不仅是对艺术家，也是对我们所有人。这个人一生都在做着准备，用四弦的小提琴演奏音乐，然而，突然间，就在音乐会进行中，他发现只剩下三根弦，于是他用三根弦演奏。那一晚他用三根弦演奏的音乐比他以往用四根弦演奏的音乐更美妙、更神圣、更难忘。所以，在我们所生活的这个动荡多变、令人困惑的世界里，也许我们的任务是就是创作音乐，起初用我们所拥有的一切，然后当这不再可能时，就用我们所剩下的来创造音乐。

本单元语言点 · 能直接搬到作文里的结构
  • be like 表比喻（Life is like a hurdle race），与 look like 区分
  • in two ways / in either case / one step closer to 三组议论文常用短语
  • make it（成功、做到）是口语高频，写作里可用 manage to do 替换
  • instead of + doing 表「而不是」，是段落转折的万能连接
  • It is one's task to do sth：it 作形式主语的典型句型
  • re- 前缀表「重新」：re-orchestrate / rebuild / reread / reconsider
真题链接　这两个比喻在阅读判断与概括段落大意里都是加分项——文章若用「比赛 / 音乐」作比喻，主旨题的答案一定落在「比喻的意义」而不是字面事件上。看到 hurdle、race、strings 这类词，先问自己：作者真正想说的是人生哪一面？
写作素材　「面对困难 / 局限 / 挫折」的最强收尾就是用帕尔曼这句话：Sometimes it is your task to find out how much you can still do with what you have left. 开头可用 300 米跨栏：Life is like a 300-meter hurdle race — the distance is the same, but the starting points are not.
10

Ode to Public Transportation

公共交通之赞歌
了解 ★
为什么这个单元重要　真题里公共交通不是高频主题，但这一单元有两个别处拿不到的东西：① 一篇标准的「四理由并列」议论文，是写环境／城市类作文的现成骨架；② Text B 换了个角度——不谈省钱省油，只谈「在公共空间里人如何对待彼此」，是「文明／尊重」这类话题少见的素材。
Text AThe Importance of Public Transportation
公共交通很重要
议论文 · 引用开篇 + 亲身经历 + 四条理由并列
主旨：作者先给公共交通下定义，再用自己在塞班岛没车只能步行的经历做铺垫，最后用四条理由（省钱、环保、减少能源依赖、提供便利）论证它为什么重要。全篇是「定义—经历—分条—总结」的标准结构。
段落骨架 · 按顺序读一遍就知道全文怎么走
①引用开篇一位美国国会议员说：毫无疑问，交通业是我们经济中最关键的行业。作者说「我很清楚他的意思」。
②时代判断在如今这个时代，能否获得交通出行已被视为一种必需品（a necessity）。
③下定义公共交通被定义为「可供一般公众使用的服务」；它是更便宜、也更环保的替代方案。
④划界它不是那种可以私下安排、只图自己方便的服务——因为你还得考虑同行的其他乘客（除非你包下火车或船上的包厢）。
⑤亲身经历作者住塞班岛（美国属地），当地只有游客巴士和校车。没有私家车或自行车，就只能租车或预付打的，否则每天上班路上大汗淋漓。
⑥细节支撑刚来时全靠步行：上下班走、去商店走、拖着沉重的杂货走回家、去教堂也走。因为那时她是刚起步的单亲妈妈，租车打的都是「一笔大开支」，所以很少去离家 20 英里以外的地方。
⑦理由 1 · 省钱据美国公共交通协会研究，把车停在家里改乘公交，每人每年可省下 9,515 美元。油价与其他车辆开销上涨，公交通勤显然省钱。
⑧理由 2 · 环保从开车改为公交，就是在减少碳足迹（carbon footprint）。若多数人都这样，对环境的改善可想而知。
⑨理由 3 · 减少能源依赖交通约占世界能源需求的 25%、每年石油消耗的 62% 以上；除铁路用电外，95% 的交通完全依赖石油产品。加强公交服务，就是在减少石油需求与依赖，也会促使我们考虑替代能源。
⑩理由 4 · 便利公交减轻了无车者与不愿买车者的负担，给他们选择；它不仅服务本地居民，也服务游客——一个没有公交的地方，游客的选择和体验都会受限。
⑪总结总而言之：公交改善生活方式、增强社区联系、为公众提供新岗位，并给我们一个更清洁的环境。
必背长难句 · 8 句（原文 + 译文 + 结构拆解）
There can be no doubt that the transportation sector is the most critical sector of our economy.
毫无疑问，交通业是我们经济中最关键的行业。
拆　There can be no doubt that... = 毫无疑问……（比 I think 更硬的判断句，写作可直接用）；sector 部门、行业；critical 关键的（此处不是「批评的」）。
In this day and age, access to transportation is considered a necessity.
在如今这个时代，能否获得交通出行已被视为一种必需品。
拆　in this day and age 在当今时代（= nowadays，更书面）；access to sth 获得…的机会／通道（to 是介词）；be considered (to be) sth 被视为。
Public transportation is defined as a service that is available for use by the general public.
公共交通被定义为一种可供一般公众使用的服务。
拆　be defined as 被定义为（名词解释题的模板句）；that 引导定语从句修饰 service；be available for use 可供使用；the general public 一般公众。
It is not a transportation service that you can arrange privately to suit your own personal convenience (although you may, to a certain extent, such as paying for a cabin or suite on a train or ship), since you have to consider other passengers who are riding and making similar arrangements with you.
它不是那种你可以私下安排、完全迁就个人方便的服务（虽然在某种程度上你可以——比如在火车或轮船上订一个包间或套房），因为你必须考虑其他乘客，他们也在乘车，也在做着和你类似的安排。
拆　此处的 that 引导定语从句（作 arrange 的宾语），不是强调句；括号内 although 引导让步状语从句，to a certain extent 在某种程度上，such as doing 举例；since 引导原因状语从句；who are riding and making... 是定语从句修饰 passengers。
According to research done by the American Public Transportation Association, individuals can save up to $9,515 annually by parking their cars at home and using public transportation instead.
据美国公共交通协会所做的研究，个人把车停在家里、改乘公共交通，每年最多可省下 9,515 美元。
拆　according to 根据；done by... 过去分词作后置定语修饰 research；save up to + 金额 最高可省…；by + doing 表方式（两个动名词并列）。
When you switch from driving your car to taking public transport, you are reducing your carbon footprint and making a great step forward in saving the environment.
当你从开车改为乘坐公共交通时，你就在减少自己的碳足迹，并在拯救环境上向前迈出一大步。
拆　switch from doing A to doing B 从 A 转向 B（from 与 to 后都接动名词）；carbon footprint 碳足迹；make a step forward in doing 在…方面前进一步。
When we strengthen our public transportation services, we are consequently lessening this oil demand and dependence.
当我们加强公共交通服务时，也就相应地减少了对石油的需求与依赖。
拆　strengthen 加强（strength + en，名词变动词的典型）；consequently 因此、相应地；lessen 减少（less + en）。
All in all, public transportation improves our way of life, strengthens the community, provides new jobs for the public and gives us a cleaner environment.
总而言之，公共交通改善我们的生活方式、增强社区联系、为公众提供新的工作岗位，并给我们一个更清洁的环境。
拆　all in all 总而言之（总结段起手式）；四个动词并列 improves / strengthens / provides / gives，是「四动词收尾」的经典写法，作文可直接仿。
核心词块 · 14 词

教材原文全文10 段 · 647 词
原文逐段排列，点右侧按钮可看中文与旁注

A US Congressman once proclaimed that,"There can be no doubt that the transportation sector is the most critical sector of our economy"I know exactly what he means. in this day and age, access to transportation is considered a necessity Public transportation is defined as a service that is available for use by the general public. It is a cheaper and environmentally friendly alternative. It is not a transportation service that you can arrange privately to suit your own personal convenience (although you may, to a certain extent, such as paying for a cabin or suite on a train or ship) since you have to consider other passengers who are riding and making similar arrangements with you.

一位美国国会议员曾经宣称，“毫无疑问，交通运输部门是我们经济中最关键的部门。”我很清楚他的意思。在当今时代，能够使用交通运输被认为是必需的。公共交通被定义为可供公众使用的一种服务。它是一种更便宜、更环保的选择。它不是能私下安排以满足个人方便的那种交通运输服务虽然从某种程度上，你能这种做，比如付钱得到火车上的包厢或轮船上的套房因为你必须考虑其他与你一起乘车并做出相似安排的乘客。

I have experienced first-hand how important public transportation is.

我亲身体验过公共交通的重要性。

I live in Sai pan, a US territory and the closest things we have to public transportation are tourist and school buses. You have to have your own car or bike, rent a car, or pay up front for a taxi to go from point A to point B. Otherwise you end up basked in sweat and sunburn traveling to work every day, When 1 first came here, I walked. I walked going to and from work, I walked to the store and lugged my heavy groceries back to the house. I walked to church and I barely got to go to places farther than a twenty-mile radius from my house since I did not own a car then, and I considered renting a car and riding a taxi such an expense for a starting single mother like I was at that time. For me, public transportation is very important because of the following reasons: 1. It saves money.

我住在塞班岛，属于美国的领土，在那里，我们所拥有的最接近公共交通的东西就是旅巴士和校车。你必须拥有自己的汽车或者自行车，或者租车，或者预付出租车费用，才能从地到B地。否则，你每天去上班最后就会被晒得汗流浃背和晒伤。刚来到这里的时候，我行。我步行上下班，步行去商店，拉着重重的食品杂货走回家。我步行去教堂，那时我几乎从来没去过离家20英外的地方因为那时我没有车，并且我认为租一辆车或者搭出租车对当时我这样一个刚开始做单身母亲的人来说是一笔太大的开支。对我来说，公共交通非常重要，理由如下:1.省钱。根据美国公共交通协会的一项研究，个人把车停在家里，乘坐公共交通出行，每年可节省高达9515美元。

According to research done by the American Public Transportation Association, individuals can save up to $9,515 annually by parking their cars at home and using public transportation instead. In this economy, saving money has become a main concern in most households. With the rising prices of fuel and other vehicle-related expenses, doing the public commute to work and school certainly saves money.

在现在这样的经济中，省钱是大多数家庭主要关心的事。随着燃油和其他车辆相关费用的上涨，乘坐公共交通去上班、上学当然省钱。

2. It helps the environment.

2.利于环境。

When you switch from driving your car to taking public transport, you are reducing your carbon footprint and making a great step forward in saving the environment. The environment costs of individuals using their cars every day has done massive damage to the environment and if the majority or all individuals like you and me use public transport instead, think of what good we could do for the environment.

当你从自己开车转变为搭乘公共交通时，你就减少了你的碳排放量，在保护环境方面迈出了一大步。个人每天开车的环境成本给环境造成了巨大的破坏，如果大多数人或所有像你我这样的人都使用公共交通，想象一下我们可以为环境带来多大的好处。

3. It will wean us out of energy dependence.

3.戒除我们对能源的依赖。

According to a paper made by Dr. Jean-Paul Rodrigue and Dr. Claude Comtois transportation accounts for approximately 25% of world energy demand and for more than 62%of all the oil used each year. Ninety-five percent of transportation is almost completely reliant upon petroleum products with the exception of railways using electrical power. While the use of petroleum for other economic sectors, such as industrial and electricity generation, has remained relatively stable, the growth in oil demand is mainly attributed to the growth in transportation demand. When we strengthen our public transportation services, we are consequently lessening this oil demand and dependence. It will also motivate us to consider alternative energy and fuel resources. 4. It provides ease and convenience.

根据珍妮-保尔·罗德里格博士和克劳德·康托瓦博士在合著的一篇论文，每年交通运输消耗的能源大约占世界能源需求量的25%，占石油使用量62%以上。95%的交通运输几乎完全依赖于石油产品，除了使用电力的铁路外。虽然石油在诸如工业和发电等其他经济部门的使用保持相对稳定，但石油需求的增长主要归因于交通运输需求的增长。当我们加强公共交通服务，我们对石油的需求和依赖就会因此而减少。这也激励我们考虑选择其它替代能源和燃料资源。4.提供轻松和便利。

Having public transportation definitely eases some of the burdens of people who do not have cars or prefer not to own one. They are provided with choices to use public transport. Public transportation also provides valuable services not just to local inhabitants in the area but to tourists as well. When a tourist visits a place, which does not have public transportation, their choices are limited and the experience they get is limited, too.

有了公共交通，必然会减轻那些没有车的人或者不愿意有车的人的负担。他们可以选择使用公共交通。公共交通不仅为当地居民，而且还为游客提供了宝贵的服务。当游客游览一个有公共交通的地方时，他们的选择就是有限的，所获得的体验也是有限的。

All in all, public transportation improves our way of life, strengthens the community, provides new jobs for the public and gives us a cleaner environment.

总之，公共交通改善了我们的生活方式，加强了社区，为公众提供了新的工作机会，给了我们一个更清洁的环境。

Text BPersonal Advantages of Taking Public Transportation
坐上公交我骄傲 · 个人层面的好处
议论文 · 换角度论证（不谈成本，谈人与人的相处）
主旨：作者不谈省钱省油，只谈一件事：公交让他看到一个「未被利益驱动的媒体扭曲」的普通人世界。在车上，人们普遍礼貌、让座、微笑、交谈——这让他相信善意是常态，也让他愿意在公共空间里做最好的自己。
段落骨架 · 按顺序读一遍就知道全文怎么走
①总起点坐公交时，我总能看到普通人的「均衡图景」——这与利益驱动的媒体所描绘的不一样。
②真实的人我遇到的是真实、可信的人，他们可能与我有同样的忧虑。
③善意假设无论在公交、火车，还是沿途载客与我拼乘的出租车上，我都得到一种确认：多数人对我是善意的，他们并不打算故意伤害我——尽管我此前遇到过几个坏人，有过可怕而粗粝的经历。
④核心主题一个重要体验：被别人尊重，也尊重别人。
⑤社会规范人们乘公交时普遍尽可能表现得礼貌、恭敬——这本来就是父母、长辈和上一代教给所有人的。
⑥公共自我在公共场合，我们始终需要呈现「最好的自己」（conduct our best selves）。
⑦具体场景看到身强体健的人给老人、残疾人、孕妇让座；甚至看到女性也给别人让座。
⑧温度看到人们微笑交谈，在公交或火车上感到人与人之间更多的暖意。
⑨落点看到人们对同乘者真诚友善，我意识到这需要很大的勇气——尤其是在艰难的时候。
必背长难句 · 6 句（原文 + 译文 + 结构拆解）
When I take public transport, I always end up seeing a balanced view of regular folks, which is different from how they're usually depicted in the profit-driven media.
当我乘坐公共交通时，我最终总会看到普通人的一幅均衡图景，而这与利益驱动的媒体通常描绘的样子并不相同。
拆　end up doing 最终（会）……；regular folks 普通人（folks = people，口语）；which 引导非限制性定语从句指代前面整句；be depicted as 被描绘成；profit-driven 利益驱动的（名词 + 过去分词构成复合形容词）。
I meet real, authentic people who may have the same concerns as mine.
我遇到的是真实、可信的人，他们可能与我有同样的忧虑。
拆　authentic 真实的、不假的（比 real 更强调「非伪装」）；the same...as... 与…相同的；concerns 忧虑、关切的事。
I get assurances that most folks mean well to me, i.e., they don't mean to harm me intentionally.
我得到一种确认：多数人对我是善意的，也就是说，他们并不打算故意伤害我。
拆　assurance 保证、确信（assure v.）；mean well to sb 对某人怀有善意；i.e. = that is 也就是说；mean to do 打算做；intentionally 故意地（≠ accidentally）。
People generally behave as politely and respectfully as they can whenever they take public transportation.
人们在乘坐公共交通工具时，普遍会尽可能表现得礼貌而恭敬。
拆　as + 副词 + as one can 尽可能……；whenever 引导时间状语从句（「每当」）；politely / respectfully 两个副词并列。
That's how all of us were taught, by and large, by our parents, our elders and the generations before.
大体上说，这正是父母、长辈以及之前世世代代教给我们的道理。
拆　That's how... 这就是…的方式（表语从句）；by and large 大体上、总的来说（插入语）；the generations before 之前的世代。
I see people behaving genuinely pleasant toward co-passengers, and I realize it takes a lot of guts to do this especially during these difficult times.
我看到人们对同车的人真心地和气，也意识到这样做需要很大的勇气，尤其是在眼下这些艰难的日子里。
拆　see sb. doing 看见某人正在做（behaving 作宾语补足语）；it takes guts to do 中 it 是形式主语，guts 是口语「胆量」；toward 表对象。

教材原文全文6 段 · 643 词
原文逐段排列，点右侧按钮可看中文与旁注

When I take public transport, I always end up seeing a balanced view of regular folks, which is different from how they're usually depicted in the profit-driven media. I meet real authentic people who may have the same concerns as mine. Whenever I'm on a bus, or the train, or eve nat times in a taxi that 1 share with other passengers, whom the driver picks up along the way, I get assurances that most folks mean well to me, i.e., they don't mean to harm me intentionally, despite a few terrible, harsh experiences I've had with some bad people before.

我乘坐公共交通时，总能看到普通人们的公正的观点，这与利润驱动的媒体对他们的描述是不同的。我遇到了真正的、真实的人们，他们跟我所关心的是同样的。虽然之前遇到过一些坏人，有过糟糕的、残酷的经历，但是每次跟其他乘客一起坐公交车、火车，甚至有时候与司机顺路搭载的其他乘客一起坐出租车的时候，我都相信大多数人对我怀有善意，也就是说不是故意要伤害我。

The meaningful experience of receiving respect from and giving respect to others People generally behave as politely and respectfully as they can whenever they take public transportation. That's how all of us were taught, by and large, by our parents, our elders and the generations before. We always need to conduct our best selves when in public. It's a great experience seeing able-bodied men giving up seats for the elderly, disabled, or pregnant wo men. L even see wo men give up seats to others. I see people move a bit on their seats just to share space to those needing seats. I see people smiling and talking with each other. I feel more warmth among people when on a bus or the train. I see people behaving genuinely pleasant toward co-passengers, and I realize it takes a lot of guts to do this especially during these difficult times. Save cash for other purposes I always get the opportunity to save money, and am actually gaining pennies these days. This is particularly true when you live in a city, like New York, where subways and buses are in place. I've done the math: it's far cheaper to take the subway and the bus, plus the ferry if you live in St aten Island like me, than to keep a car and drive yourself to your usual destination. You save on the parking fees, the toll fees, the insurance cost, the repair and maintenance costs and the perceived costs of aggravation from motorists on the road, the fuel, among other costs. On public transportation, you simply just pay the fare.

受到他人尊重并尊重他人的有意义的经历人们乘坐公共交通出行的时候，通常是尽可能礼貌待人，尊重他人，总的来说，我们的父母、长辈以及前几代人都是这么教育我们的。在公众场合，我们总是需要表现最好的自己人让座。我还看到有些人在座位上稍微挪动了一点点，给那些需要座位的人腾出一点空间。我到身体健壮的人给老年人、残疾人、或者孕妇让巫，是一次很棒经历。我甚至看到女士给1看到人们微笑着，相互谈论着。在公交车或者火车上，我在人群中感到更温暖。我看到人们对其他乘客表现出真实的友善，我意识到这需要很大的勇气，尤其是在这些困难时期。省钱做其他用途我总是能找到机会省钱，实际上这些天也攒了一些。当你住在像纽约这样有铁和公交的城市里，尤其如此。我计算过:如果你像我一样住在斯塔顿岛，乘坐地铁和公交车、再加上海也比拥有车并自己开车去通常的目的地要便宜得多。你节省了停车费、通行费、保险费、维修保养费、以及在路上驾驶者感到加重的成本:燃料，还有其他费用。乘坐公共交通，你只要付车费就行了。

Light baggage to bring with you I just carry myself and my tiny baggage. I occupy the least space, which is actually wherever I happened to be standing or sitting, which contributes to my baggage being very economical and lighter to carry. If ever there would be untoward incidents while in transit, always be assured that l only have myself to worry about, which is unlike when you're in your car. If an accident happens while you are in your car, you will be doubly aggravated because you will have to keep tabs on things other than yourself (who, by then, may be extremely anxious or even, may the Lord forbid, in a worst situation).

随身携带轻便的行李我只带我自己和我的小行李。我占据最少的空间，也就是我碰巧站着或坐着的地方，这样有助于我的行李变得经济和更轻便。即使在途中出现了一些意外事故，我总是确信我只需要扣心我自己就可以了，不像你坐在车里。如果你在车里时发生了事故，情况就会加倍恶化，因你要密切注意的不仅是你自己，还有别的事到那时候，你可能非常焦虑，甚至在最坏的下，但愿这种事情不会发生。

Strong push for long-term environmental projects I am confident that I contribute more to efforts in sustaining and keeping a much cleaner environment. Motorists always have to consider that they actually are contributing more to accumulated environmental damage when they use fossil-sourced fuel for their cars. Those who are concerned about the environment, especially policy makers, leaders and managers, might as well heed this by taking the chance to take public transportation if time allows them. Their followers will eventually get the message: you'll be contributing more to saving what's left of our habitat by keeping away from using less of the natural resources.

对长期环保项目的强力推动我确信自己为维护和保持更加清洁的环境做出了更多努力。开车的人总是要考虑，他们的汽车使用化石燃料，这实际上是加大了累积的环境破坏。在时间允许的情况下，那些关心环的人，尤其是政策制定者、领导和经理，最好还是注意一下这个问题，通过找机会乘坐公共通。他们的追随者最终会得到这样的信息:通过避免使用较少的自然资源，你将为拯救我们存的家园做出更大的贡献。

Movement and exercise I get the chance to do my exercises walking from my place to the bus stand (or the subway station, or the ferry station). I actually feel a much health ier me whenever I take public transportation.

运动和锻炼我有机会做锻炼，从家里步行到公交站或者地铁站，或者轮渡站，每次乘坐公共交通出行，我都会感觉自己更健康了。

Come, join me and everyone else taking public transportation.

快来，与我和其他人一起乘坐公共交通吧。

本单元语言点 · 能直接搬到作文里的结构
  • end up doing（最终会…）；mean to do（打算）；be defined as（被定义为）
  • switch from doing A to doing B：from 与 to 后都接动名词
  • see sb doing sth（看到某人正在做）——感官动词后接现在分词表进行，接原形表全过程
  • as + 副词 + as one can = 尽可能……
  • it takes guts / courage / time to do sth：it 作形式主语的固定句型
  • 名词 + 过去分词构成复合形容词：profit-driven / man-made / well-known
  • strengthen / lessen：形容词或名词 + -en 变动词（wide→widen, strength→strengthen）
真题链接　公共交通不是近两年的真题主题，但「环境」「城市生活」类文章常作为阅读判断的背景材料。这类文章的判断陷阱集中在数字与程度：原文说 some cities，题干说 all cities，答案为 B（错误）；原文只给方向没说数量，题干给出具体数字，答案为 C（未提及）。Text A 里那组数字（9,515 美元、25%、62%、95%）就是典型的「出题点」。
写作素材　「环境保护 / 绿色生活」类作文可直接搬用：Public transportation is a cheaper and environmentally friendly alternative. It saves money, reduces our carbon footprint and lessens our dependence on oil. 结尾用原句改写：All in all, it improves our way of life, strengthens the community and gives us a cleaner environment. 若题目是「文明 / 尊重」，用 Text B 的角度：On a bus, people behave as politely as they can — that is how we were taught by our parents and elders.
11

Cyber World

网络世界
必背 ★★★
为什么这个单元重要　2024 年 10 月的写作真题就是 Uses of the Internet——这一单元直接命中。Text A 提供了一个很好用的角度：网络把「先看外表再看内在」的顺序颠倒过来了；Text B 则是「利弊类」作文的标准结构：正—反—合，结尾用一句思想实验（想象网络突然消失的世界）收束。那串五个动词并列的句子（improved / simplified / offered / enabled / provided）可以直接搬进任何一篇「好处列举」的作文。
Text ACyber Love
网络情缘也能修成正果
议论文 · 举例 + 对比 + 争议
主旨：网络恋爱不是「不正常」，而是把恋爱的顺序颠倒了：现实中先看外表再见内心，网上先交出内在、赢得之后再暴露外表。它的核心特征是「交流占比极高」，而亲密感的养成需要长时间。
段落骨架 · 按顺序读一遍就知道全文怎么走
①开场案例90 年代末，丹麦的 Maria 与荷兰的 Martin 通过聊天热线认识，聊了几个月、互发邮件，相距 700 公里仍决定见面。见面很成功，此后一直幸福地生活在一起，还建了主页讲述他们如何通过网络找到幸福。
②历史视角自古以来男女用过各种方式寻找另一半。有人认为网恋怪异，但对 90 年代出生的人来说，这是找潜在伴侣的自然方式。
③共同脚本网上发展的感情有一个典型剧本：亲密感的培养是漫长过程，常要几个月。「一见钟情」罕见。
④推进机制最初是轻浅的交流（邮件或聊天室），随后话题越来越自我暴露，再过一阵，两个陌生人把彼此当成真正的朋友。
⑤情绪强度心门一开，邮件如雪崩般穿过网络空间，带着文学气质，还配上诗句与虚拟礼物（鲜花、香吻、动画）。任何有浪漫倾向的心如何抵挡？
⑥关键差异网恋与现实恋爱的最大不同在于「交流的占比」。现实中约会前会梳妆打扮；网上约会时，我们展现的是智慧、想象力与个性。
⑦顺序颠倒本质上这与现实约会的顺序相反：我们先展示内在美，只有在内在赢得之后，外表才被揭示。
⑧争议热议话题：在没有外表吸引、甚至不知对方长相的前提下，能否产生真正的爱情？换句话说，如果两人已因思想与情感而相爱，外表还起作用吗？
⑨反方许多人说，看不见表情、举止与身体语言的爱不符合爱的本性；支持者则坚持，这样发展出的感情强度高于因肉体激起的感情。
必背长难句 · 7 句（原文 + 译文 + 结构拆解）
Throughout history men and women had used a variety of means to find each other.
自古以来，男人和女人用过各种各样的方式来寻找彼此。
拆　throughout history 纵观历史（写作开头的时间状语）；a variety of = various 各种各样的；had used 过去完成时，表「在网恋出现之前早已如此」。
Internet romance, according to some, is a bizarre method, and to others it is a natural way for the 90's to meet potential mates.
在有些人看来，网恋是种怪诞的方式；而在另一些人看来，它是 90 年代人结识潜在伴侣的自然途径。
拆　according to some 插入语；and to others 平行对比；a way for sb to do sth 某人做某事的方式；potential mates 潜在伴侣。这句是「双视角立论」的模板。
Romances formed on the internet follow a characteristic script.
在网上形成的恋情遵循一套典型的剧本。
拆　formed on the internet 过去分词短语作后置定语修饰 romances；follow a script 遵循剧本（比喻「有固定套路」）。
The initial light exchanges, whether by e-mail or in chat rooms, are generally followed by increasingly self-revealing topics, and then after a while, the two strangers perceive each other as a true friend.
最初那些轻浅的交流——无论是通过邮件还是在聊天室——通常会被越来越自我表露的话题接续，再过一段时间，两个陌生人便把彼此看作真正的朋友。
拆　be followed by 被…接续（被动，表顺承）；whether...or... 让步状语插入语；perceive sb as sth 把某人看作。
Hearts open and an avalanche of e-mail crosses cyberspace carrying literary quality, and electronic messages are even enhanced with verses and virtual gifts.
心门打开，邮件如雪崩般穿过网络空间，带着文学气质；电子信息甚至用诗句和虚拟礼物来增色。
拆　an avalanche of 雪崩般的（量词比喻）；carrying... 现在分词作伴随状语；be enhanced with 用…增强。
When you reach for the mouse with sweaty palms and butterflies in the stomach to look in the in-box for new mail — there's just no way to escape the fact — love has arrived.
当你手心冒汗、心里七上八下地去点鼠标、查看收件箱里的新邮件时——这个事实已无从逃避——爱情已经来了。
拆　reach for 伸手去拿；with sweaty palms and butterflies in the stomach 独立主格式的伴随状语（butterflies in one's stomach = 心里七上八下）；there's no way to escape the fact 无从逃避这个事实。
While before a real date we fix our hair and our clothes, on the internet we polish our intellect, imagination and personality.
现实中约会之前我们先打理头发和衣服；而在网上，我们打磨的是才智、想象力和个性。
拆　While 表对比；两个分句结构对称（before a real date / on the internet），是「网恋顺序相反」的关键句。

教材原文全文6 段 · 726 词 · 旁注 9
原文逐段排列，点右侧按钮可看中文与旁注

Thanks to the late 20th century technology, Maria of Denmark and Mar ti jin of Holland met on a chatline. For months they've talked and sent letters to each other with the help of their computers as they found themselves falling head over heels in love. It was then that they decided to meet in the real, not only the virtual, world. It was not easy to arrange as the young man and his lady were separated by 700 very real kilometers, but the date was a success and Maria and Martijn have been living together happily ever since. They've created a home page to let the world know how they've found happiness via the Internet and introduce couples who've met under similar circumstances. Throughout history men and wo men had used a variety of means to find each other. Internet romance, according to some, is a bizarre method, and to others it is a natural way for the 90's to meet potential mates.

由于20世纪末的科技进步，丹麦的玛丽亚和荷兰的马汀通过聊天热线相识了．他们彼此心生爱慕，数月以来他们不断通过电脑聊天，互发邮件。最终，他们决定在现实世界中见上一面，而不仅仅是在虚拟的网络世界里相识。不过，这个年轻人和他心仪的女人相隔足足700公里，要见面也并非易事，但是约会最终却非常成功，而且从那之后玛丽亚和马汀一直幸福地生活在一起。他们还建立了自己的主页，告诉全世界的人们他们是如何通过网络找到幸福的，也介绍那些有着和他们类似经历的情侣们。有史以来，男人和女人们通过各种各样的方法来找寻自己的另一半。对于有些人来说，网络爱情有些怪诞，但对于90年代后出生的人来说，它是找寻潜在对象的一种自然而然的方法。

Romances formed on the internet follow a characteristic script （剧本） . The development of emotional intimacy is a long process, sometimes taking several months."Love at first byte （字节） " is rare although there are examples. The initial light exchanges, whether by e-mail or in chat rooms, are generally followed by increasingly self-revealing topics, and then after a while, the two strangers perceive each other as a true friend. Hearts open and an avalanche of e-mail crosses cyber space carrying literary quality, and electronic messages are even enhanced with verses and virtual gifts (flowers, kisses, animated pictures). Could any heart with romantic inclinations resist? When you reach for the mouse with sweaty palms and butterflies in the stomach to look in the inbox （收件箱） for new mail - there's just no way to （无法做） escape the fact - love has arrived. How does a cyber romance vary from a real life romance? Perhaps one significant difference is that communication plays such a large part. Words that convey feelings have enormous effect. While before a real date we fix our hair and our clothes, on the internet we polish our intellect, imagination and personality. In essence, the sequence is reversed - first we show our inner beauty and only after it had won victory does the veil fall off the physical vehicle of that personality at the time of that first meeting in person.

网络上发展的爱情通常有一个共同特点：培养亲密感是一个漫长的过程，有时要花上好几个月的时间。尽管也会有一见钟情的事例，但毕竟是少数。不管是通过电子邮件还是聊天室，最初都是简单的交流，然后是越来越多自我表露的话题，再过了一段时间之后．两个陌生的人会把彼此当成真正的朋友。一旦敞开心扉，充满文学素养的电子邮件会如雪崩般的纷至沓来，电子留言也会充满诗意，并带有像鲜花、香吻和动画的虚拟礼物。但凡任何有点浪漫情怀的心如何能够抵挡住这样的攻势?当你手心冒汗，心里七上八下地用鼠标点击收件箱的新邮件时，恐怕这时你就必须承认这个事实了，爱情已经悄然而至。那么网络爱情和现实生活中的爱情到底有什么不同呢?可能其中一个重要的不同就在于交流占据了很大一部分。表达感情的言语会产生巨大的影响力。在现实的约会之前，我们会梳妆打扮一下，但是在网上约会的时候，我们通常做的是充分展现自身的智慧、想象力和个性。从本质上讲，这和现实中的约会的顺序是相反的：我们先展示了内在美，只有在内在美赢得胜利之后，第一次见面时外在的特点才展露出来。

It is a popular topic in cyber space to debate whether or not true love can develop merely on an intellectual level, without physical attraction or the familiarity of the other's appearance? To put it another way: is the physical appearance of the man or woman play a part in the relationship if, through the exchange of thoughts and feelings, they already fell in love?

在网上，关于在没有外表吸引或在不知晓对方外表的前提下而仅在精神层面上能否产生真正的爱情是一个热议的话题，换一种说法就是：如果通过思想和情感上的交流，男人和女人已经相爱了的话，他们的外表在爱情关系中还起作用么?许多人说，没有视觉感受，不能在当场看到对方的表情、举止和身体语言的爱不符合爱的本性。

Many say it is against the nature of love to be deprived of the sense of sight, the look, the movement, and the body language being present. There are those, of course, who favor Internet relationships claiming that the intensity of the emotional relationship that develops in such a wa yis superior to the mere stirring of the flesh.

当然，那些支持网络爱情的人会坚称，以这样的方式发展的感情强度要高于因肉体激起的感情。

Whatever the opinion may be on cyber love - it must be said in favor of the internet that it puts people in touch with those who they otherwise would never have met. I would never have suspected that the first person I met by e-mail would be a German chimney sweeper, with whom, after a year of correspondence, albeit not romantic, a common interest still exists. It is silly to perceive the Net as a demon （恶魔） , when we determine how we meet its challenges. While there are those who can use the Net to their advantage extracting useful information, there are those who become addicts losing common sense, hopping from chat room to chat room, writing piles of e-mails full of lies to chosen victims, and whose virtual reality （虚拟实境） , the flirtations （调情） , become part of their everyday lives, as if a disease. One thing is true - the Net is very addictive. According to the confession of a multiple substance addict, it was easier to give up cocaine than the IRC （shortforInternetRelayChat网上实时聊天） .

不管人们对网络爱情的观点如何，关于网络，有一点是应该承认的，那就是网络使那些可能永远不会谋面的人互相结识。我从来没有想到过我通过电子邮件认识的第一个人会是一个德国的烟囱清洁工，在经过一年的书信联系之后，尽管不是很浪漫，但我们之间还是相互感兴趣的。当我们决心面对网络带来的挑战时，也不用把网络想象成一个妖魔。有些人能使用网络获取有用的信息，而有些人却得了“网瘾”失去理智，在不同的聊天室穿梭，写了成堆的满是谎话的邮件去欺骗那些被他们选为目标的受害者，他们虚拟的现实——在网上调情．成了他们日常生活的一部分，犹如疾病缠身一样。不过，有一点的确是真的，那就是网络会让你着迷。根据一个吸食多种毒品的瘾君子的交代，戒掉可卡因要比戒掉“网瘾”容易些。

In any case, keep your eyes open off-line as well. Turn off the machine and go to a dance or a club, because most wo men still expect to be courted （求爱） in a real, old-fashioned, and romantic way.

不管怎么样，下线了也要留心，关掉电脑，去跳跳舞，或是参加个俱乐部，因为大部分的女人还是喜欢真实的、传统的、浪漫的追求方式。

Text BThe Impact of the Internet on Society
先给好处，再给代价，最后一句反问
议论文 · 双面论证（正—反—合）
主旨：结构极其工整，是「利弊类」作文最标准的模板：先讲互联网从「政府项目」变成「大众新鲜物」再变成「绝对必需品」，然后一段讲正面（五个动词一口气铺开），一段讲负面（沟通变差、失去耐心、时间被吞掉），最后合——pros and cons 并存，但有一点确定：影响是巨大的。要证明这一点，只需想象一下网络突然消失的世界。结尾「把时钟拨回去」（turn back the clock）是全文最漂亮的一笔。
段落骨架 · 按顺序读一遍就知道全文怎么走
①总起 · 时间尺度过去二十年，互联网对社会产生了深远影响。虽然它最初是政府运作的项目，但随着时间推移，这项创新技术最终演变成了大众的新鲜事物。
②今昔对比看今天的社会，你会发现情况确实变了。对很多人来说，互联网已成为日常生活的一项必需品，而不再是十年前那个好玩的新鲜物。
③十年前的判断甚至就在十年前，如果你去想互联网对社会有没有重大影响，答案很可能是「大概没有」。虽然网络可能被视为一项很棒的革新，但并不像有谁「需要」它。
④转折 · 认知变了然而随着时间推移，这一认知在社会中许多人身上改变了。互联网在很大程度上已成为绝对的必需品——无论是出于要求，还是出于愿望。
⑤常态化的三个证据上网变得像人们传统上做的其他日常事务一样自然。人们理所当然地认为网络会一直可用；对「保持连接」的依赖已成为常态。企业和个人同样依赖互联网的日常可用性。
⑥为什么必须 24/7由于许多企业、政府机构和其他组织都已迁移到在线环境，人们一般需要持续访问——或者说 24/7（一天 24 小时、一周 7 天）访问，如果你愿意这么说的话。
⑦正面 · 主题句在很多方面，互联网对社会有着非常积极的影响。
⑧正面 · 五连动词它改善了沟通，简化了任务与琐事的处理，提供了巨大便利，实现了更快的处理速度，并提供了更多选择——所有这一切都释放出更多时间去做别的事。
⑨负面 · 主题句然而，有趣的是，互联网在某些方面也带来了负面影响。
⑩负面 · 三条沟通质量下降了；人们变得没有耐心，因为他们已习惯于「即时满足」；而使用互联网本身也占用了大量时间。
⑪负面 · 具体化随着 Facebook 等网站和其他基于社交的互动使用量增加，人们每天要花很多小时去维护自己的各种网络，这挤占了其他活动的时间。
⑫合 · 承认两面从后一个角度看，人们会怀疑互联网是否真的造福了社会。现实地讲，跟任何事物一样，互联网有其正反两面（pros and cons）。
⑬合 · 好处仍巨大电子邮件和即时通讯这类工具已成为标准；社交网络正迅速演变成下一层次的沟通方式；人们享受着持续不断的连接。在这方面，互联网的好处是巨大的。
⑭证据这不仅体现在人们联网时所从事的众多活动中，也体现在智能手机等移动设备的持续进步与发展上。
⑮社会已改变预期社会已经演进到这样的程度：人们期待并依赖随手可得的信息。人们在做生意和处理个人事务时也期待得到快速回应。互联网让这一切成为可能。
⑯收束 · 归因当今社会视为理所当然的许多事物，都是互联网的发展使之成为可能的。
⑰终极一问互联网对社会究竟产生了什么样的影响？包裹在优点与缺点之中的是：有一点是确定的——互联网对社会产生了显著的影响。
⑱思想实验要想象这种影响，你只需试着设想一下：如果互联网突然从社会中被移除，后果会是什么。想象一个互联网无法使用的世界。
⑲落点它会怎样影响你？从这个角度去看，对很多人来说，这大概会让人看清：社会已变得多么依赖互联网——而要把时钟拨回去（turn back the clock），会极其困难。
必背长难句 · 16 句（原文 + 译文 + 结构拆解）
The Internet has had a profound effect on society over the last two decades.
过去二十年里，互联网对社会产生了深远的影响。
拆　has had —— 现在完成时，配合 over the last two decades（过去二十年间）这个标志性时间状语，是本课第一号时态考点。have an effect on sth 对……有影响（= affect v. = influence v.）。profound 深远的、深刻的（比 big 高级得多）。two decades 二十年（decade = 十年）。
Although it began as a government operation, over time this innovative technology eventually evolved into a public novelty.
虽然它最初只是一个政府运作的项目，但随着时间推移，这项创新技术最终演变成了大众的新鲜事物。
拆　although 引导让步状语从句，主句前不再加 but（中英差异的高频错点）。begin as 以……的身份/形式开始。over time 随着时间的推移。innovative 创新的（innovate v. / innovation n.）。evolve into 演变成（evolve from 由……演化而来）。novelty 新奇的事物（novel adj. 新奇的）。
Looking at society today, one may find that the situation has certainly evolved.
看看今天的社会，人们会发现情况确实已经变了。
拆　Looking at... 现在分词短语作状语（逻辑主语是 one）。one may find that... 人们会发现……（one 作泛指主语，比 we 更书面）。certainly 确实、无疑（此处是强调，不是「当然」）。
Even a decade ago if you were to think about whether or not the Internet had significant impact on society, the answer would likely have been probably not.
甚至在十年前，如果你去想互联网对社会是否有过重大影响，答案很可能是「大概没有」。
拆　if you were to do 是虚拟条件句（were to 表「设想中的将来/假设」，语气最弱、最客气）。whether or not 是否（= whether...or not，此处直接连用）。had significant impact on —— 完成时表「截至那时的既有影响」。would likely have been 是虚拟主句的完成式。这句连用 were to / would have been，是全课语法密度最高的一句。
While the Net may have been considered a neat innovation, it was not like anyone "needed" it.
尽管网络可能被视为一项很棒的革新，但并不像有谁「需要」过它。
拆　while 引导让步状语从句（= although，放句首更常见）。may have been considered 是「情态动词 + 完成式被动」，表对过去的推测。neat 在此是口语「很棒的、绝妙的」（不是「整洁的」）。it was not like... 口语结构，表「并不像是……」。
People take it for granted that the Internet will be available, and dependency on being connected has become the norm.
人们理所当然地认为网络会一直可用；而对「保持连接」的依赖已经成为常态。
拆　take it for granted that... 想当然地认为……（it 是形式宾语，that 从句是真宾语——这是形式宾语的头号考点）。be available 可用的、可获得的。dependency on sth 对……的依赖（depend on → dependent → dependency）。being connected 动名词被动式作介词宾语。the norm 常态、常规。
Due to the fact that many businesses, government agencies and other organizations have navigated to the online environment, generally people require consistent access, or 24/7 access, if you will.
由于许多企业、政府机构和其他组织都已迁移到在线环境中，人们一般需要持续访问——或者说 24/7 的访问，如果你愿意这么讲的话。
拆　due to the fact that = because（更书面的「因为」，写作中可用来避免重复 because）。navigate to 导航到、迁移到（本义「航行」，此处引申）。require 需要（比 need 正式）。consistent 持续的、一贯的。if you will 插入语，「如果你愿意这么说的话」，用于给一个不够严谨的说法打圆场。24/7 = 24 hours a day, 7 days a week，全天候。
It has improved communication, simplified handling tasks and errands, offered huge convenience, enabled faster processing, and provided more options, all of which have freed more time to do other things.
它改善了沟通，简化了任务与琐事的处理，提供了巨大的便利，实现了更快的处理速度，并提供了更多选择——所有这些都释放出更多时间去做别的事。
拆　全课最值得背的句子，写作「好处列举」的最强模板：五个动词并列（improved / simplified / offered / enabled / provided），句式整齐、气势足。handling tasks and errands 中 handling 是动名词作定语修饰 tasks。enable faster processing 使更快的处理成为可能（enable + 名词）。all of which 引导非限制性定语从句，指代前面整串并列内容——这是 which 指代「前面一整件事」的标准用法。
The quality of communication has declined; people are impatient because they have become accustomed to instant gratification, and using the Internet also takes up a lot of time.
沟通的质量下降了；人们变得没有耐心，因为他们已习惯于「即时满足」；而使用互联网本身也占用了大量时间。
拆　decline 下降、衰退（不及物，此处不用被动）。be accustomed to sth/doing 习惯于（to 是介词，后接动名词 —— 高频错点）。instant gratification 即时满足（心理学术语，写作提分词）。take up 占用（时间、空间）。分号连接三个并列分句，比用 and 更紧凑，是书面议论文常用的标点手段。
As websites such as Facebook and other socially based interactions increase in usage, many hours a day are consumed as people keep up with their various networks, taking away from other activities.
随着 Facebook 等网站及其他基于社交的互动使用量增加，人们每天有许多小时被消耗在维护自己的各种网络上，从而挤占了其他活动的时间。
拆　As... 引导时间/伴随状语从句（「随着」）。such as 例如（后接名词，不能接句子）。increase in usage 使用量增加（increase in + 名词表「在……方面增长」）。are consumed 被消耗（被动）。keep up with 跟上、维护（keep up with the news 跟进新闻）。taking away from... 现在分词短语作结果状语（「从而挤占了」）。
Realistically, as with anything else, the Internet has its pros and cons.
现实地讲，跟任何其他事物一样，互联网有其正反两面。
拆　Realistically 现实地说（副词作句首评注语）。as with sth 正如……一样（as with = as is the case with）。pros and cons 优点与缺点（固定搭配，复数形式，写作万能词）。这句是「辩证收尾」的标准句，可直接套用。
This is not only evidenced by the many activities people engage in when they connect to the Internet, but also by the continuous progression and development of mobile devices such as smartphones.
这一点不仅被人们在联网时从事的众多活动所证明，也被智能手机等移动设备的持续进步与发展所证明。
拆　not only... but also... 连接两个 by 短语（并列的是状语，因此不倒装；若连接主语则需注意主谓一致）。be evidenced by 被……所证明（evidence 作动词 = 证明）。people engage in 是省略 that 的定语从句修饰 activities。progression 进步、演进（比 progress 更强调「过程」）。
Society has evolved to the point where people expect and rely on readily available information.
社会已经演进到这样的程度：人们期待并依赖随手可得的信息。
拆　to the point where... 到了……的程度（where 引导定语从句修饰 point，这是 point / stage / case 后接 where 的固定用法）。rely on 依赖（= depend on）。readily available 随手可得的（readily = easily）。
Wrapped up in the advantages and disadvantages one thing is for certain: the Internet has had a significant effect on society.
包裹在优点与缺点之中的是：有一点是确定的——互联网对社会产生了显著的影响。
拆　Wrapped up in... 过去分词短语作状语（「被包裹在……之中」）。one thing is for certain = one thing is certain（for certain 是「确切地」的固定搭配）。冒号后是同位语式的解释。这句的倒装感来自把状语提到句首，主语 one thing 后置——写作里用来「破开平铺直叙」很有效。
To imagine the impact all one has to do is try to envision the consequences if the Internet was suddenly removed from society.
要想象这种影响，你只需要试着设想一下：如果互联网突然从社会中被移除，后果会是什么。
拆　all one has to do is (to) do sth —— all 作主语、后接定语从句时，系动词后的不定式常省略 to（这是本句的语法核心）。envision 设想、预想（= imagine，更书面）。be removed from 被从……中移除。if 从句用 was removed 表假设。
Looking at it from this respect, for many it likely brings to light just how reliant and dependent society has become on the Internet and it would be extremely hard to turn back the clock.
从这个角度去看，对很多人来说，这大概会让人看清：社会已经变得多么依赖互联网——而要把时钟拨回去，会极其困难。
拆　Looking at it from this respect 现在分词短语作状语。bring sth to light 使……显露、揭示。how reliant and dependent society has become on... 是 how 引导的宾语从句（用陈述语序；reliant / dependent 两词近义叠加，起强调作用）。it would be extremely hard to do sth 做某事会极其困难（it 形式主语）。turn back the clock 把时钟拨回去、让时光倒流 —— 全篇的收尾金句。

教材原文全文3 段 · 598 词 · 旁注 6
原文逐段排列，点右侧按钮可看中文与旁注

The Internet has had a profound effect on society over the last two decades. Although it be gan as a government operation, over time （随着时间的转移, 逐步） this innovative technology eventually evolved into a public novelty. Looking at society today, one may find that the situation has certainly evolved. For many, the Internet has become an essential for daily living, no longer the fun novelty it was 10 years ago. Even a decade ago if you were to think about whether or not the Internet had significant impact on society, the answer would likely have been probably not. While the Net may have been considered a neat innovation （绝妙的革新, neat意为很棒的, 绝妙的） , it was not like anyone"needed" it. However, over time this perception has changed for many individuals in society. The Internet has become, for the most part （总体上） , an absolute necessity, either by requirement or by desire. Going online has become as natural as doing other daily tasks people have traditionally done. People take it for granted that the Internet will be available, and dependency on being connected has become the norm. Both businesses and individuals alike depend upon the routine availability of the Internet. Due to the fact that many businesses, government agencies and other organizations have navigated to the online environment, generally people require consistent access, or 24/7 (24 hours a day, 7 days a week) access, if you will.

在过去20年的时间里，网络对社会产生了巨大的影响。尽管网络最开始的时候还只是由政府运作，不过随着时间的推移，这项创新科技最终变成了大众的新鲜事物。看看当今社会，我们会发现这种情况也早已大为不同。对于很多人来说，网络已变成了日常生活的必需品，而不再是10年前的新鲜事物。即使是十年前要你想网络对社会是不是有巨大的影响，你很可能会回答不是。尽管网络已经被看作是一项革新，但不是每个人都需要它。然而，对社会上大多数人而言，这个观念在一段时间以来已经发生了变化。在极大程度上来说，网络已经彻头彻尾地变成了必需品，不管是客观上要求，还是主观上需求都是这样。上网变成了自然而然的事情，就像是人们习惯做的其他的日常工作一样。对于网络的存在，人们都习以为常；对它的依赖也变成了一种常态。商业人士和普通人都要依赖网络来完成日常的活动。许多公司、政府机关以及其他组织都已经实现网上办公，人们一般要求随时能够访问他们的网站，或者，全天都能够访问他们的网站，如果你愿意的话。

In many ways the Internet has had a very positive effect on society. It has improved communication, simplified handling tasks and errands, offered huge convenience, enabled faster processing, and provided more options, all of which have freed more time to do other things. Yet, interestingly enough the Internet has, in some ways, had negative effects. （主题句） The quality of communication has declined; people are impatient because they have become accustomed to instant gratification, and using the Internet also takes up a lot of time. As websites such as Facebook and other socially based interactions increase in usage, many hours a day are consumed as people keep up with their various networks, taking away from other activities. When looking at it from the latter perspective, one may wonder whether or not the Internet has truly benefited society. Realistically, as with （正如） anything else, the Internet has its pros and cons. Tools such as e-mail and instant messaging have become the standard; social networks are quickly evolving into the next level of communication, and people enjoy their constant access. In this respect the benefits of the Internet are tremendous. This is not only evidenced by the many activities people engage in when they connect to the Internet, but also by the continuous progression and development of mobile devices such as smart phones.

网络在很多方面对社会起到了积极作用。它提高了沟通的效率，简化了处理工作的任务，提供了方便，加快了数据处理的速度，也提供了更多的选择，所有这些都可以节省出更多的时间去做别的事情。然而，有意思的是，网络在很多方面也有消极的影响。沟通的质量下降了，另外由于习惯了网络上的瞬时反应速度，人们变得没有耐心了，同时网络也耗费了我们大量的时间。随着比如脸谱和其他社交网站的使用量的增加，人们每天耗费数个小时忙于各种网络联系，占用了其他活动的时间。从网络消极的影响上来看，人们可能会想网络到底对社会有没有益处。实际上，所有的事情都是这样，网络也有它的正反面。电子邮件和即时消息这样的工具成了普遍的事物，社交网络正在迅速地演变成下一个层次的沟通方式，人们享受的是时时刻刻的联系。在这一点上，网络的好处是巨大的。人们利用互联网所从事的种种活动，以及诸如智能手机等移动设备的持续进步和发展都能证明这一点。

Society has evolved to the point where people expect and rely on readily available information. People also expect to receive rapid responses when doing business and personal tasks. The Internet makes this possible. Many of the things that society takes for granted nowadays are all made possible by the development of the Internet What kind of an impact has the Internet had on society? Wrapped up in the advantages and disadvantages one thing is for certain: the Internet has had a significant effect on society. To imagine the impact all one has to do is try to envision the consequences if the Internet was suddenly removed from society. Imagine a world where the Internet was unavailable. How would it affect you? Looking at it from this respect, for many it likely brings to light just how reliant and dependent society has become on the Internet and it would be extremely hard to turn back the clock （时光逆转） .

社会已经发展到这样一个阶段，人们希望得到并且依赖即时的信息，人们也希望在处理生意和私人事务时能够得到快速的反馈。网络使这些都成为可能。许多当今社会习以为常的事情其实都是因为网络的发展才变成可能的。那网络到底对社会产生了什么样的影响呢?优点也好，缺点也罢，有一点是肯定的：网络已经对社会产生了巨大的影响。想知道影响到底有多大，我们仅需要想象一下把网络从社会中突然剥离后的结果就可以了。想象一下一个没有网络的世界吧。它会影响你什么呢?从这个角度上考虑的话，很多人就会明白我们的社会是多么依赖网络，要是真回到过去的话该是多么艰难的事情。

本单元语言点 · 能直接搬到作文里的结构
  • only + 状语置于句首 → 部分倒装：Only after winning on the inside do we reveal the outside.
  • be followed by 表顺承（被动结构），与 be based on 区分
  • whether...or... 引导让步状语从句，可作插入语
  • perceive A as B / regard A as B / think of A as B：三个「把…看作」
  • an avalanche of / a flood of / a sea of：三个比喻量词，写作提分
  • have an impact on = affect = influence，三者可互换但词性不同
  • has had / over the last two decades：现在完成时 + 过去二十年间的标志性状语
  • take it for granted that...：it 作形式宾语，that 从句是真宾语（形式宾语头号考点）
  • be accustomed to doing：to 是介词，后接动名词
  • to the point where...：point / stage / case + where 引导定语从句
  • all of which：which 指代前面一整串并列内容（非限制性定语从句）
  • not only...but also... 连接状语时不倒装，连接主语时才需注意主谓一致
  • if you were to do / would likely have been：虚拟条件句的最弱语气形式
真题链接　2024.10 写作真题就是 Uses of the Internet，本单元可以直接供料。2023.10 填词补文出现过 internet 一词。写作时注意：题目要「uses」，就写 3 个用途 + 1 句辩证收尾，不要写成「利弊分析」——跑题是写作最常见的失分点。
写作素材　写 Uses of the Internet 的万能骨架：The Internet plays an important role in my daily life. I use it for studying online, keeping in touch with friends and family, and handling daily tasks such as shopping and banking. Of course, it is a tool rather than a value — what matters is how we use it. 这段可直接套 2024.10 的真题。
12

A Break from Life

偷得浮生半日闲
重点 ★★
为什么这个单元重要　Text A 是一篇难得的「自我觉察」散文，语言极口语化，长难句密度低，很适合当阅读判断的练手材料；它也是「如何减压 / 如何平衡生活」类作文的现成范本。
Text AFeeling Free
放松一下 · 感受自由
第一人称散文 · 心理流动 + 自我对话
主旨：作者某天起床就烦躁，什么都不想干，于是给自己「制造」头痛、关节酸、要感冒的症状，好名正言顺地回床上。最后她承认：我其实只是想要一天假。她不再需要「生病」这个借口，而是允许自己休息。
段落骨架 · 按顺序读一遍就知道全文怎么走
①情绪醒来就烦躁（cranky）：不想做家务（衣服已堆成山），不想读从办公室带回来的材料，不想做任何「像负责任的行为」。
②身体化喝早茶时觉得头痛要来了——「是的，就在眼睛后面隐隐作痛」，也许该回床上躺到它消退。
③自我诊断洗碗时肌肉开始酸痛？还是关节？那可能意味着要得流感了——今年人人都得了流感，凭什么我能躲过？我绝对该躺床上。
④回到床上她钻回被窝闭眼，可已经完全清醒。该起床了，但头痛、开始流鼻涕——还是去拿纸巾吧。
⑤合理化阅读抱着家庭装纸巾盒路过时，顺手拿上那本买了却没时间读的新小说；靠着枕头读起来，一读二十页。
⑥继续找理由该去啃那份报告了，至少该起来洗衣服；可万一我传染呢？衣服可以等，家人有办法凑出明天穿的衣服。
⑦承认也许我并不是真的要得流感。我其实并不想生病。说实话，我只是想要一点点属于自己的时间——我需要把自己从人群、家务、职业和外部世界里「养」回来。
⑧童年对照小时候，能不上课、不做家务的唯一理由就是生病；但我不再是孩子了。难道我必须制造症状，才能给自己一个借口吗？不，我决定了，我不需要。
⑨自我对话我和自己谈话：好，你需要休息一天。承认它，扔开负罪感，去享受一个小假期。你想做什么？读书？你已经在读了。纵容自己？泡个泡泡浴。当个隐士？让机器接电话。
⑩行动倒了半瓶浴液、加了一把洋甘菊浴盐，点上香草蜡烛，小心地踏进浴缸，满足地叹了口气，把自己泡进这个自制的水疗。
⑪效果电话在远处响，她笑了。奇怪的是，热水里那些酸痛消失了，随最后的泡泡一起滑进下水道；头也不痛了，搏动被一种安宁取代。
⑫升华傍晚她回来了，身体、精神、情绪都焕然一新。不是感到无助，而是感到被赋能——她允许自己倾听并回应自己的需要，像照顾家人那样照顾自己。她不需要「生病」这根拐杖来为休息正名。这是多么简单的领悟，可不正是简单的东西让我们自由吗？
必背长难句 · 9 句（原文 + 译文 + 结构拆解）
I woke up feeling cranky. I didn't want to do housework, though the laundry was piling up.
我醒来时心情烦躁。我不想做家务，尽管要洗的衣服已经堆成了山。
拆　wake up feeling + 形容词（现在分词作伴随状语）；though 引导让步状语从句（此处 though 在句中，等于 although）；pile up 堆积。
It was that kind of day.
就是那样的日子。
拆　极简句。that kind of 表「那种（说不清但都懂的）」，是英文散文里营造语气的高效短句。
As I put the dishes in the sink, it seemed that my muscles were beginning to ache. Or was the ache in my joints?
当我把碗放进水槽时，似乎肌肉开始酸痛了。还是酸痛在关节里？
拆　as 引导时间状语从句（「当…时」）；it seemed that... 表推测；begin to do 开始做。
Did I have to manufacture symptoms to provide myself with an excuse? No, I decided, I didn't.
我非得编造出症状，才能给自己一个借口吗？不，我决定了，我不需要。
拆　manufacture 本义「制造」，此处引申为「捏造」（熟词僻义，考点）；provide sb with sth 提供给某人某物；I decided 作插入语。
To be truthful, all I wanted was a little time off. I needed to nurture myself away from people, chores, career and the outside world.
说实话，我想要的只是一点点休息时间。我需要把自己从人群、家务、职业和外部世界里「养」回来。
拆　to be truthful 说实话（插入语）；all I wanted was... 主语从句 + 表语；nurture oneself away from 远离…滋养自己；chores 家务。
As a child, the only respite from school or family chores was illness.
小时候，能逃开学校或家务的唯一喘息机会就是生病。
拆　as a child 作为孩子时（= when I was a child）；the only respite from... 逃离…的唯一喘息；respite 暂缓、间歇。
I had given myself permission to listen and respond to my needs, to care for myself the way I tended to my family.
我允许自己去倾听并回应自己的需求，像照顾家人那样照顾自己。
拆　give oneself permission to do 允许自己做（写作高分结构）；respond to 回应；the way (that) I tended to my family 方式状语从句（the way 后省略 in which）。
I didn't need the crutch of illness to justify a rest.
我不需要「生病」这根拐杖来为休息正名。
拆　the crutch of illness 疾病这根拐杖（比喻）；justify 为…提供正当理由（justification n.）。
It was such a simple awareness, but then isn't it the simple things that set us free?
这是多么简单的领悟，可不正是那些简单的东西让我们自由吗？
拆　such a + 形容词 + 名词；but then 不过话说回来（转折插入）；isn't it...that... 是 It is...that... 强调句的反问形式，用于结尾反问。

教材原文全文8 段 · 601 词 · 旁注 24
原文逐段排列，点右侧按钮可看中文与旁注

I woke up feeling cranky （脾气坏的） . I didn't want to do housework, though the laundry was piling up. I didn't want to read the work I brought home from the office. I didn't want to do anything that resembled responsible behavior. It was that kind of day.

一醒来我的心情就很糟糕。我不想做家务，尽管要洗的衣服已经堆积如山。我也不想理会那些我从办公室带回家的工作。我不想做任何负责任的事。这就是那样的一天。

As I drank my morning tea, I thought I felt a headache coming on. Yes, there it was, a dull throb （跳动） just behind my eyes. Maybe I should go back to bed until it subsided （消退） . As I put the dishes in the sink, it seemed that my muscles were beginning to ache. Or was the ache in my joints? That could mean I was coming down with the flu. Everyone I knew had the flu this year. Why should I be the one to escape （免于） it? I absolutely should be in bed.

当我喝早茶的时候，我就感到阵阵头痛袭来。是的，就是，一阵隐隐约约的疼痛充斥着我的眼睛。或许，我应该继续睡觉直到疼痛消失。当我把盘子丢进水槽里的时候，我的肌肉好像也开始隐隐作痛。或许是我的关节在痛?那是不是我得了流感了呢?我认识的每个人今年都得了流感。凭什么我幸免于此?我的确应该躺在床上。

I shuffled （拖步走） back to bed, wiggled （扭动） under the covers and shut my eyes.

我拖拉着回到床上，在被子下扭动了下就闭上了眼睛。

Another couple of hours of sleep would be so nice, but I was already completely awake. I ought to get up. But no, there was that headache and the beginning of a sniffle （吸鼻子） . Better get the tissues.

能再睡几小时就好了，可我现在已经完全清醒了。我应该起床。不，还是不要了。我的头还痛着，鼻子也开始抽抽搭搭了，我最好去拿些纸巾。

On my way back from the bathroom with a family-sized （家用的） tissue box, I stopped to grab that big new novel I had bought but had no time to read. I opened the book and settled （安顿） against the pillows.

我去浴室取了一盒家庭装的纸巾，在回到卧室的路上，我停下来抓起那本我早买了但一直没有时间看的大部头小说。我靠在枕头上打开了书。

The morning was moving along and so was my reading. Another twenty pages and I was stretching. I should try to crack （找到解决...方法） the report I was working on. I should at least get up and do the wash. What if I was contagious? I certainly didn't want to spread any germs. The wash could wait. My family was resourceful enough to scrounge （索取） clothing for the next day. Maybe I wasn't actually getting the flu. I didn't really want to be sick. To be truthful, all I wanted was a little time off. I needed to nurture （养育） myself away from people, chores （琐事） , career and the outside world. Did I have to wait to be sick to do that? As a child, the only respite （暂停） from school or family chores was illness. But I wasn't a child any more. Did I have to manufacture （捏造） symptoms to provide myself with an excuse? No, I decided, I didn't. I talked to myself. Okay, I said, you need a day off. Admit it. Accept it. Toss out （扔掉） the guilt and enjoy a mini-vacation. What would you like to do? Read? You're already doing that. Pamper （纵容,放纵） yourself? Take a bubble bath. Be a hermit （隐士） ? Let the machine answer the phone.

一早上就这样过去了，我的阅读也这样继续着。接着我又看了20页，我伸展了一下身体。我应该把那个我一直忙着的工作报告做完。或许，我至少应该起床，洗点衣服。要是我患了传染性感冒怎么办?我可不想传播细菌啊。那些脏衣服可以等等再洗。家人有足够的能力找到第二天能穿的衣服。或许我根本就没有感冒。我其实也并非真想生病。说实话，我只是想要一点闲暇的时间而已。我想让自己远离人群、琐事、职业以及外面的世界。我就非得等到生病了才能那样吗?孩子能不上课或不做家务的唯一方法就是生病，但我已经不再是个孩子了。我不得不装出生病的症状来给自己找借口吗?不，我决定了，我不需要。我和自己交谈，好的，我说，你需要休息一天。承认吧，接受吧，抛掉罪恶感，去享受这个小假期。那你想做些什么呢?阅读吗?你已经那么做了啊。要不就纵容一下自己，洗个泡泡浴?还是就做个隐士，让答录机去接电话呢?我在水中倒了半瓶沐浴露，还加了一大把甘菊浴盐，然后点起一支香草味的蜡烛，轻手轻脚地走进了浴缸里。

I poured half the bottle of bath gel into the streaming water and added a hearty （大的） handful of chamomile （春黄菊） bath salts. Then I lit a vanilla-scented （香草味的） candle and gingerly stepped into the bathtub. With a grateful sigh, I immersed myself in my homemade spa. I heard the phone ring somewhere off in the distance and smiled.

我长舒了一口气，泡起了自制的水疗。我听见远处电话响起，笑而不应。真是有意思，在热气腾腾的浴缸里，那些疼痛竞消失得无影无踪了，它们都随着最后的泡泡流进了下水道里。

It is funny how the aches subsided in the heat of the tub. They just slipped away with the last of the bubbles down the drain. My head felt just fine, the throb replaced by a sense of well-being. By late afternoon, I was back at it （恢复原样） , refreshed physically, mentally and emotionally. And rather than feeling helpless, I felt empowered. I had given myself permission to listen and respond to my needs, to care for myself the way I tended to （注意,留心） my family. I didn't need the crutch （拐杖） of illness to justify a rest. It was such a simple awareness, but then isn't it the simple things that set us free?

我的头感觉好多了，眼痛也被美好的感觉取而代之。下午晚些时候，我恢复了精神，身体上，精神上，情绪上全都焕然一新。我也不再感到无助，反而感觉到充满力量。我给了自己倾听并满足自己需求的机会，像照顾家人一样关心了一下自己。我根本不需要生病这样的理由去休息。这其实是一个非常简单的意识，不过难道不是那些简单的事情让我们感到自由吗?

Text BSelf-esteem and Body Image
爱上自己 · 自尊与身体形象
说明文 · 问题 + 归因 + 建议（面向青少年）
主旨：「我太胖 / 我太瘦 / 我要是个子再高点就好了」——几乎每个人都对自己说过。青春期身体剧变，自我形象随之变化，自尊跟着起落。关键是分清「我的身体」和「我的价值」。
段落骨架 · 按顺序读一遍就知道全文怎么走
①共鸣开场「我太胖。我太瘦。我要是个子再高一点、再矮一点、头发再卷一点、再直一点、鼻子再小一点、肌肉再多一点、腿再长一点就好了。」——这些话听着耳熟吗？你是不是习惯了贬低自己？如果是，你并不孤单。
②为什么发生作为青少年，你的身体正在经历大量变化；身体变化时，你对自己的形象认知也在变。很多人难以适应，而这会影响自尊。
③为什么重要自尊影响选择、关系、学习表现与心理健康；身体形象是自尊最容易被撬动的一块。
④媒体因素广告与社交媒体提供的是经过筛选与修图的「标准」，与之比较注定失败。
⑤建议 1把「我的身体」和「我的价值」分开：身体是载体，不是评分。
⑥建议 2关注功能而非外观：这双腿能走路、这双手能做事——功能视角比外观视角稳定得多。
⑦建议 3减少触发源：取关让你自我怀疑的账号，比强迫自己「正能量」有效。
⑧落点健康的目标不是「变成别人」，而是「照顾好现在的自己」。
必背长难句 · 5 句（原文 + 译文 + 结构拆解）
I'd be happy if I were taller, shorter, had curly hair, straight hair, a smaller nose, bigger muscles, longer legs.
我要是个子再高一点、再矮一点，头发再卷一点、再直一点，鼻子再小一点，肌肉再多一点，腿再长一点，我就会开心了。
拆　虚拟语气：if I were（be 动词一律用 were）+ 过去式 had；六个比较级并列，是「永远不满意」的排比式呈现。
Do any of these statements sound familiar? Are you used to putting yourself down? If so, you're not alone.
这些话听起来耳熟吗？你是不是习惯了贬低自己？如果是，你并不孤单。
拆　sound familiar 听起来耳熟（sound 作系动词 + 形容词）；be used to doing 习惯于做（≠ used to do 过去常常）；put sb down 贬低某人；if so 如果是这样。
As your body changes, so does your image of yourself.
随着你的身体发生变化，你对自己的印象也随之改变。
拆　as 引导时间状语从句（「随着」）；so does + 主语 = 「…也一样」，是完全倒装，写作提分句型。
Lots of people have trouble adjusting, and this can affect their self-esteem.
很多人难以适应，而这会影响他们的自尊。
拆　have trouble (in) doing sth 做某事有困难（trouble 后接动名词）；affect 是动词（effect 是名词，易混）。
Body image is how someone feels about his or her own physical appearance.
身体形象指的是一个人对自己外貌的感受。
拆　how 引导表语从句；his or her 避免性别偏向。
